Local search can target active lag terms: choose active score orientation, pick a term with high contribution and perform pair move that locally reduces it. This is like steepest descent.

Efficient continuous local search algorithm:
1. Initialize h, scores S.
2. Iteration:
   - Find active scores: indices where S >= Smax - tol (e.g., 0.01). Could maintain top indices by np.flatnonzero; O(n).
   - For a random active index k (score), compute d and orientation. If k=0, self term: individual h_i contributes h_i(1-h_i); to reduce, push h_i to 0 or 1? But sum must preserve; pair with some j. Could handle separately.
   - For plus lag d: score S_p = ∑_{r=0}^{m-1} h_r(1-h_{r+d}) in sum units. Terms contributions c_r = h_r(1-h_{r+d}). Pick weighted random r with probability c_r (or largest). Proposed move: i=r, j=r+d, δ>0 small? We can choose optimal δ for this term with bounds but ensure not harm others? Maybe do line search over δ values (e.g., 0.1,0.2,0.5*max, extremes) or solve 1D min max for all scores. Simpler: pick δ = min(0.1, 0.5*h_i, 0.5*(1-h_j)) or a few candidates (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5). Evaluate S update for each, choose best max, accept if improves.
   - If not improve, try negative direction (move from j to i) or random pair.
   - Also random global pair moves to escape.

Let's design algorithm:
- For each n (choose even: 70 parent, 140, 210 perhaps), start from best h.
- Projected gradient (PG) with adaptive α (logsum temp), step η. Use exact F for acceptance (not smooth) or F_α.
- Periodically re-evaluate scores, select active, use exact max F.
- If no improvement for many iterations, change α, step, random perturb, or move to SLSQP.

Let's design final program in detail.

Main functions:
- compute_c5(h): exact discrete max with clip, sum check? For returned h must feasible. Use evaluate_erdos_solution? Pre-imported maybe returns? We can just compute. But to ensure exact, use np.correlate as task.
- feasible_h(h,n): clip, adjust sum using bisection+residual.
- score_sum(h): returns S vector length 2n-1 in sum units; C = max(S)*2/n. Need efficient. We can compute directly using loops over d? For n<=200, maybe use np.correlate plus prefix for plus/minus like previous score_max. Let's implement robust:
For h length n, target sum n/2. Let m=n.
S[0] = ∑ h_i(1-h_i) = sum_h - sum_h2.
For d in 1..n-1:
 m=n-d
 S[2d-1] = ∑_{i=0}^{m-1} h_i(1-h_{i+d}) = prefix[m] - dot(h[:m], h[d:])
 S[2d] = ∑_{i=0}^{m-1} h_{i+d}(1-h_i) = (total - prefix[d]) - dot(h[:m], h[d:])

Initialize G=zeros(n)
For each index idx, weight w:
 if w<=tiny continue.
 if idx==0: G += w*(1-2h)
 else:
  d=(idx+1)//2 if odd else idx//2
  m=n-d
  if idx%2==1: # plus
    G[:m] += w*(1 - h[d:])
    G[d:] -= w*h[:m]
  else: # minus
    G[d:] += w*(1 - h[:m])
    G[:m] -= w*h[d:]
Return G.

def pg_step(h, S, U, alpha, eta, n):

  # scale G? step in sum-units. Use eta.

    S_new=score_sum(z); F_new=max S_new
    if F_new < U - 1e-11:
       return z, S_new, F_new, eta
    eta*=0.5
  return None

Let's write score_sum carefully:
```
def score_sum(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    n=h.size
    S=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    if n==1:
       S[0]=float(h[0]*(1-h[0]))
       return S
    ac=np.correlate(h,h,mode='full')
    # ac[n-1+d] = sum_{i=0}^{n-1-d} h_i h_{i+d}
    pref=np.empty(n+1); pref[0]=0; np.cumsum(h,out=pref[1:])
    total=float(pref[n])
    S[0]=total - float(ac[n-1])
    for d in range(1,n):
       m=n-d
       a=float(ac[n-1+d])
       S[2*d-1]=pref[m] - a
       S[2*d]=total - pref[d] - a
    return S
```
This loop O(n) with Python for n=140 fine. Could vectorize d arrays:
```
d=np.arange(1,n)
m=n-d
a=ac[n-1+d]
S[1::2? indices odd 1,3...?] = pref[m] - a
S[2::2] = total - pref[d] - a
```
Need indices: plus index 2d-1 for d=1..n-1 => indices 1,3,...,2n-3 (odd excluding last?) length n-1. minus 2,4,...,2n-2. We can assign:
```
S[1:2*n-1:2] = pref[m] - a
S[2:2*n:2] = total - pref[d] - a
```
This is fast. Let's verify S length 2n-1, last index 2n-2. For d=n-1, plus idx=2n-3, minus idx=2n-2. Good. S[0] self.
Feasible h should total target but if slightly not, formula still.

def objective_score_gradient(h, alpha):
   S=score_sum(h)
   m=max(S)
   if alpha <= 1e-12:
      tol = max(1e-9, 1e-8*m)
      act=np.flatnonzero(S >= m - tol)
      w = np.zeros_like(S); w[act]=1/len(act)
   else:
      z=(S-m)/alpha
      # avoid overflow: if max z maybe 0, min large negative
      p=np.exp(z) ; p/=p.sum()
      w=p
   G=...
   return S, m, G
```

def pg_refine(h,n,state,deadline,time_limit,alpha_list):
   h=make_feasible(h,n); S=score_sum(h); U=max(S); update.
   best=U; best_h=h.copy()
   eta=0.1
   for alpha in alpha_list:
      start=time
      no_decrease=0
      no_global=0
      while time<deadline and no_decrease<500:
         S=score_sum(h); U=max(S); if U<best... update
         if time - last_best > 20: break? 
         compute weights/gradient; if normG < 1e-10: break
         step = eta / max(0.5, np.linalg.norm(G)) maybe.
         improved=False
         for attempt in range(8):
             z=project_box_sum(h - step*G, target)
             S2=score_sum(z); U2=max(S2)
             if U2 < U - 1e-10:
                 h=z; S=S2; U=U2; if U<best...; eta=min(1.0, eta*1.2); improved=True; break
             step *=0.5
         if improved:
             no_decrease=0
         else:
             no_decrease +=1
             eta = max(0.01, eta*0.8)
             if no_decrease % 100 ==0:
                 # random perturb
                 pert = rng.normal(0,0.05,n); pert -= pert.mean(); z=project(h+pert); S=score_sum(z); U=max; if U < U_best? but may increase. accept if U< best*1.001? We can accept to escape even if worse? Need maintain best. Could accept if U < U + 0.05 else revert.
```

Let's detail make_feasible robust for n large:
```
def make_feasible(raw, n):
    h=np.array(raw,float, copy=True)
    if h.size != n: resize/truncate/resp? We'll call with correct.
    h=np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    s=h.sum()
    if abs(s-target) <=1e-12: return h
    # distribute residual to variables not at bounds weighted by distance? Use bisection add const then residual.
    lo=-1; hi=1
    for _ in range(60): mid... s_mid=np.clip(h+mid).sum(); if s_mid<target lo=mid else hi=mid
    h=np.clip(h+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    return adjust_residual(h,target)
```
Previous adjust_residual may loop adding to all non-bound; could be slow? n small. Good.

`adjust_residual`: after bisection residual small <1e-8, adjust one element maybe enough. Simpler:
```
def adjust_residual(h,target):
   h=np.clip(h,0,1).copy()
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-14 return
   if diff>0:
      idx=np.argmax(1-h)
      h[idx]=min(1,h[idx]+diff)
   else:
      idx=np.argmax(h)
      h[idx]=max(0,h[idx]+diff)
   # If still due to bound, distribute
   for _ in range(n):
      diff=target-h.sum()
      if abs(diff)<1e-14 break
      if diff>0:
         mask=(h<1-1e-15)
         if not np.any(mask): break
         cnt=mask.sum(); inc=min(diff/cnt, 1-h[mask].min())
         h[mask]+=inc
      else: ...
   return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
This one-index can overshoot if target far >max bound, but bisection makes small. Good.

Need be careful: target may be .5 for odd; mask okay.

Now, score_sum uses h possibly not exactly sum target; objective still okay. But SLSQP constraint keeps.

Projected gradient uses target n/2, project each step. Good.

SLSQP constraint: Use `constraint_eq(x)` returns sum(x)-target; but if x size changes? We can use x.size.
```
def slsqp_constraint_eq(x):
    n=x.size; return float(np.sum(x)-0.5*n)
def slsqp_constraint_jac(x):
    n=x.size; return np.ones(n)
```
SLSQP expects constraint jac shape (m,n) possibly; returning 1D okay? It usually accepts array shape (1,n) or (n,) for single constraint. Use `np.ones((1,n))` to be safe.

SLSQP objective/gradient need alpha and maybe n? x size. Use `global _RUN_DEADLINE` check. For alpha, if x not finite return large.
```
def slsqp_objective(x, alpha):
   check_deadline()
   h=np.clip(x,0,1)
   S=score_sum(h); m=float(np.max(S))
   if alpha<=1e-14: return m
   z=(S-m)/alpha; A=np.sum(np.exp(z)); if not finite... return m + alpha*log(A)
```
But S contains scores for h not necessarily sum target? SLSQP enforces; okay. For alpha, gradient uses weights. To avoid recomputing S in gradient if not cached, okay.

return G
```

We need `softmax_weights_scores(S, alpha, thresh)`:
```
def softmax_weights(S, alpha, thresh=1e-9):
    L=S.size
    m=np.max(S)
    w=np.zeros(L)
    if alpha<=1e-12:
       tol=max(1e-10, 1e-8*abs(m))
       act=np.flatnonzero(S>=m-tol)
       w[act]=1.0/len(act)
    else:
       z=(S-m)/alpha
       # to avoid all underflow if z min very negative, exp okay (0)
       p=np.exp(z)
       s=p.sum()
       if not finite or s<=0: p=zeros; p[argmax]=1
       else p/=s
       w=p[p>thresh]
    return w
```
But for gradient, if we threshold, weights sum <1; should renormalize to sum1? If omit small weights, direction magnitude changes. We can renormalize: wsum=w.sum(); if wsum>0: w/=wsum. For exact active threshold, okay.

Objective value: use exact max S, not soft, for acceptance. The direction from soft with alpha. For α small, gradient points to rough max.

`project_box_sum` code:
```
def project_box_sum(v, target):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=v.size
    if target<=0: return np.zeros(n)
    if target>=n: return np.ones(n)
    vmin=v.min(); vmax=v.max()
    lo=-vmax - 1.0
    hi=1.0 - vmin + 1.0
    # ensure sums
    for _ in range(40):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=np.sum(np.clip(v+mid,0,1))
       if s<target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    z=np.clip(v+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    return adjust_residual(z,target)
```
If lo/hi not bracketing due v? At lo=-vmax-1, v+lo ≤ -1 for max, all <=? Some v smaller may be -1? Actually if v=[0,1], vmax=1, lo=-2, v+lo=[-2,-1] sum0. hi=1-vmin+1: if vmin=0, hi=2, v+hi=[2,3] sum n. Bracket. Good.

def update_scores_pair(S, h, i, j, a, n):
    if a == 0: return
    b = -a
    hi=h[i]; hj=h[j]
    S0 = S.copy? We'll modify S in place. S[0] += 2*a*(hj - hi)  # deriv: a(1-2hi)+b(1-2hj)=a(1-2hi)-a(1-2hj)=2a(hj-hi)
    for d in range(1,n):
        m = n - d
        dp = 0.0
        # plus i
        if i < m:
            dp += a * (1.0 - h[i+d])
        if i >= d:
            dp -= a * h[i-d]
        if j < m:
            dp += b * (1.0 - h[j+d])
        if j >= d:
            dp -= b * h[j-d]
        S[2*d-1] += dp
        dm = 0.0
        if i >= d:
            dm += a * (1.0 - h[i-d])
        if i < m:
            dm -= a * h[i+d]
        if j >= d:
            dm += b * (1.0 - h[j-d])
        if j < m:
            dm -= b * h[j+d]
        S[2*d] += dm
        if j - i == d or i - j == d:
            cross = a*b? Wait cross = -Δ_i Δ_j = -a*b. Since b=-a, -a*b=a^2. Use -a*b to be safe.
            S[2*d-1] += -a*b
            S[2*d] += -a*b
```

I forgot self product is quadratic. Earlier I treated linear but for S0 it is quadratic. For single variable change Δh, S0 change = Δ(1-2h) - Δ^2. For pair, S0 change = a(1-2hi) - a^2 + b(1-2hj) - b^2 = a(1-2hi)+b(1-2hj) - (a^2+b^2). With b=-a: linear 2a(hj-hi) -2a^2. For example a=-0.5: linear1 -0.5=0.5 correct. def targeted_local_search(h,n,state,deadline,time_limit,rng):
   h=make_feasible; S=score_sum; U=max; update; bestU=U
   no_improve=0
   active_tol=0.05  # sum units
   while time<deadline:
      # recompute active if no improve or periodically
      if no_improve % 50 ==0 or not hasattr: act=flatnonzero(S>=U-0.05)
      best_move=None; best_S=None; best_U=U
      for _ in range(5):
         i,j,a = sample_move(...)
         if invalid continue
         S_test=S.copy()
         update_scores_pair(S_test,h,i,j,a,n)
         # maybe clip small negative scores? due errors; max unaffected.
         newU=max(S_test)
         if newU < best_U - 1e-10:
             best_U=newU; best_move=(i,j,a); best_S=S_test
      if best_move:
         h[i]+=a; h[j]-=a; h=np.clip(h,0,1); S=best_S; U=best_U; accepted++
         if U < bestU_global... state.update
         if accepted%200==0: S=score_sum(h); U=max; if U<state... 
         no_improve=0
      else:
         no_improve+=1
         if no_improve>200:
             # perturb small
             h = random_perturb(h,n,rng,0.05)
             S=score_sum(h); U=max
             act=...
             no_improve=0
             # allow temporary worst? keep best state
         if no_improve>2000: break
   final update
```

def sample_target_move(h,S,U,n,act,rng):
   if act.size==0: idx=argmax(S)
   else: idx=act[rng.integers(act.size)]
   if idx==0:
      c=h*(1-h); pos=nonzero(c>1e-10); if none return None
      i=pos[rng.integers(len(pos))]
      j=int(rng.integers(n))
      if i==j: return None
      if h[i] > 0.5: maxδ=min(h[i],1-h[j]); a=-positive*f
      else: maxδ=min(1-h[i], h[j]); a=positive*f
      return i,j,a
   d=...
   if idx%2==1:
       m=n-d
       i0=r; i1=r+d
   else:
       i0=r+d; i1=r
   # choose r
   r = rng.integers(m)
   # optionally if h[i0]*(1-h[i1]) low, try argmax? Could compute c and max if source active. For speed, use r weighted by maybe h[i0] and (1-h[i1]) but no need.
   hi0=h[i0]; hi1=h[i1]

   if rng.random()<0.8:
       maxδ=min(hi0, 1-hi1)
       if maxδ<=1e-8: use opposite or random
       f=fractions[...]
       a=-maxδ*f
   else:
       maxδ=min(1-hi0, hi1)
       a=+maxδ*f
   # To avoid too large causing global increase? We can test; line search candidate.
   return i0,i1,a
```

   # choose toward extremes? maybe a = (low+high)*0.5 + ...

def random_smooth_h(n, rng, scale=0.25, anti=False):
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
   y=np.zeros(n)
   for k in range(1,8):
      amp=rng.normal(0, 0.2/k)
      y += amp*np.sin(np.pi*k*(x-1.0) + rng.normal(0, 2*np.pi))  # may not anti but mean not zero
   h=0.5+scale*y? Actually y after adding already. Use h=0.5 + 0.4*np.tanh(y)

def upsample_linear(parent, n):
   if parent.size==n return parent.copy()
   x0=(np.arange(parent.size)+0.5)*(2.0/parent.size)
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
   return np.interp(x,x0,parent.astype(float))
```

def random_perturb(h,n,rng,sigma):
   p=rng.normal(0,sigma,n); p-=p.mean()
   z=project_box_sum(h+p, n/2.0)
   return z

Now, random smooth/block candidate functions.
```
def random_block_h(n,rng):
   h=np.zeros(n)
   i=0; bit=int(rng.integers(2))
   while i<n:
      length=int(rng.integers(2, max(3, min(10, n//2+1))))
      if i+length>n: length=n-i
      h[i:i+length]=bit
      i+=length; bit=1-bit
   # smooth
   k=np.array([0.2,0.6,0.2])
   out=np.convolve(h,k,mode='same')[:n]
   return make_feasible(out,n)
```
`random_wave_h(n,rng)`:
```
x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)-1.0
y=np.zeros(n)
for f in (1,2,3,5):
   y += rng.normal(0, 1.0/f)*np.sin(math.pi*f*x + rng.normal(0, 2*math.pi))
h=0.5 + 0.45*np.tanh(y)
return make_feasible(h,n)
```

def remaining_time(deadline):

       t2=_RUN_DEADLINE - t
       if t2 < deadline - t: return max(0.0,t2)

`slsqp_objective`:
```
def slsqp_objective(x, alpha):
   check_deadline()
   h=np.asarray(x,float)
   n=h.size
   if n==0 or not all finite: return 1e6
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   S=score_sum(h); m=float(np.max(S))
   if alpha<=1e-13: return m
   z=(S-m)/alpha
   # For alpha small, z min maybe -inf; exp ok. z max 0.
   p=np.exp(z); A=float(np.sum(p))
   if not finite or A<=0: return m
   val=m + alpha*math.log(A)
   return val
```
If alpha large, z min maybe -100, exp underflow but A≥1. Good.

   return G

Now, targeted `update_scores_pair` with b=-a. Let's write exact:
```
def update_scores_pair(S, h, i, j, a, n):
    if a == 0.0: return
    b = -a
    hi = float(h[i]); hj = float(h[j])
    S[0] += a*(1.0 - 2.0*hi) + b*(1.0 - 2.0*hj) - a*a - b*b
    for d in range(1,n):
       m = n - d
       dp=0.0
       if i < m:
          dp += a*(1.0 - h[i+d])
       if i >= d:
          dp -= a*h[i-d]
       if j < m:
          dp += b*(1.0 - h[j+d])
       if j >= d:
          dp -= b*h[j-d]
       idxp=2*d-1
       S[idxp] += dp
       dm=0.0
       if i >= d:
          dm += a*(1.0 - h[i-d])
       if i < m:
          dm -= a*h[i+d]
       if j >= d:
          dm += b*(1.0 - h[j-d])
       if j < m:
          dm -= b*h[j+d]
       S[idxp+1] += dm
       if j - i == d or i - j == d:
          cross = -a*b  # = a^2 (since b=-a)
          S[idxp] += cross
          S[idxp+1] += cross
```
Potential double cross if i,j pair for d both plus/minus? yes once. If j-i = d or i-j = -d. If i==j (a=0) no. Good.

Test n=2 h=[1,0], i=0,j=1,a=-0.5,b=0.5. Now, targeted active sample: For plus idx, c=h[:m]*(1-h[d:]). If choose r, i0=r, i1=r+d. To reduce plus term, a negative (decrease i0). Our sample uses a=-maxδ*f. Good. For minus idx, c=h[d:](1-h[:m]) corresponds r term h_{r+d}(1-h_r); i0=r+d, i1=r. a negative decreases i0 (h) and increases i1. Good.

Feasible range: if a negative, low=max(-h[i], h[j]-1), high=min(1-h[i], h[j]). For a = -δ, need δ≤h[i], δ≤1-h[j]. This matches high? a≥ -h[i] and a≤h[j]-1? 0≤h_i+a≤1 => -h_i ≤ a ≤ 1-h_i.
0≤h_j-a≤1 => h_j-1 ≤ a ≤ h_j.
Intersection low=max(-h_i, h_j-1), high=min(1-h_i,h_j). For negative a, need a≥low (which might be h_j-1 if h_j<1? h_j-1 negative) and a≤0. max δ for a=-δ: δ≤h_i and δ≤1-h_j => a≥-min(h_i,1-h_j) = low? low = max(-h_i, h_j-1)= -min(h_i,1-h_j). yes. high≥0 usually. So clamp works.

Now, `targeted_local_search` details in code. Need passes state, deadline, time_limit but we can ignore time_limit and use deadline. Maintain best_h, best_U, best_S? If current h (best_h) improved. After stage return best_h.

Pseudo:
```
def targeted_local_search(h0,n,state,deadline,rng):
    h=make_feasible(h0,n); S=score_sum(h); U=float(np.max(S)); C=U*2/n
    state.update_fast(h,C,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_U=U; best_C=C
    act=None; act_counter=0; no_improve=0; escape=0
    while in_budget(deadline):
       if act is None or no_improve >= 100 or act_counter>200:
          act_tol=max(0.02, 0.002*U)
          act=np.flatnonzero(S >= U - act_tol)
          if act.size==0: act=np.array([int(np.argmax(S))])
          act_counter=0
       best_move=None; best_S_test=None; best_U_test=U + 1e-8
       escape_move=None; escape_U=U
       for _ in range(5):
          mv = sample_target_move(...) or random
          if mv None: continue
          i,j,a=mv
          low=max(-h[i], h[j]-1.0); high=min(1-h[i], h[j])
          if low >= high: continue
          a=min(max(a,low),high)
          if abs(a)<1e-12: continue
          S_test=S.copy()
          update_scores_pair(S_test,h,i,j,a,n)
          newU=float(np.max(S_test))
          if not math.isfinite(newU): continue
          if newU < best_U_test:
             best_U_test=newU; best_move=(i,j,a); best_S_test=S_test
          if newU < best_U + escape_tol:
             escape_move=(i,j,a); escape_U=newU; escape_S_test=S_test
       if best_move is not None and best_U_test < U - 1e-9:
          i,j,a=best_move; h[i]+=a; h[j]-=a; clip; S=best_S_test; U=best_U_test; C=U*2/n
          if U < best_U - 1e-10: best_U=U; best_C=C; best_h=h.copy()
          state.update_fast(h,C,n) # maybe not too often
          no_improve=0; act=None; act_counter=0
          accepted_count+=1
          if accepted_count%200==0:
             S=score_sum(h); U=float(np.max(S))
             C=U*2/n
             if U<best_U: best...
             state.update_fast(h,C,n)
       elif no_improve > 150 and escape_move is not None:
          # accept escape if not too bad
          i,j,a=escape_move; h[i]+=a; h[j]-=a; S=escape_S_test; U=escape_U; C=...
          no_improve=0; act=None
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve > 1500:
             h=best_h.copy(); S=score_sum(h); U=best_U; no_improve=0; act=None
    # final update
    h=make_feasible(best_h,n); S=score_sum; U=max; C=...
    state.update(h,C,n)
    return best_h
```
Need `escape_tol`. Sum units; U ~26. Escape increase of 0.05 okay? Too small. Use 0.2 maybe. For n=140, 0.2 corresponds C 0.0014. Use `escape_tol = max(0.05, 0.005*U)` ~0.13. But accepting worse may wander. We only if stuck 150. Good.

Need ensure `escape_S_test` not None. If escape move not lower than U but less U+tol. Good.

`sample_target_move` uses `act`. If `idx==0`, call `sample_self_move` maybe. Let's implement within function to avoid separate.

```
def sample_target_move(h,S,U,n,act,rng):
   if act is None or act.size==0: idx=int(np.argmax(S))
   else: idx=int(act[rng.integers(act.size)])
   if idx==0:
      # pick index with large h(1-h)
      c=h*(1-h)
      pos=np.flatnonzero(c>1e-10)
      if pos.size==0: return None
      i=int(pos[int(rng.integers(pos.size))])
      # pick partner with room in wanted direction
      if h[i] > 0.5:
         partners=np.flatnonzero(h < 0.99)
         direction=-1
      else:
         partners=np.flatnonzero(h > 0.01)
         direction=1
      if partners.size==0: return None
      j=int(partners[int(rng.integers(partners.size))])
      if i==j: return None
      if direction==-1:
         maxd=min(h[i], 1.0-h[j])
      else:
         maxd=min(1.0-h[i], h[j])
      if maxd <=1e-8: return None
      f=float(_FRACTIONS[int(rng.integers(len(_FRACTIONS)))])
      a=direction*maxd*f
      return (i,j,a)
   d=...; m=n-d
   # compute c
   if idx%2==1: c=h[:m]*(1-h[d:])
   else: c=h[d:]*(1-h[:m])
   tot=float(np.sum(c))
   if tot <= 1e-12: return None
   csum=np.cumsum(c)
   th=rng.random()*tot
   r=int(np.searchsorted(csum, th, side='left'))
   if r>=m: r=m-1
   if idx%2==1:
      i0=r; i1=r+d
   else:
      i0=r+d; i1=r
   hi0=float(h[i0]); hi1=float(h[i1])
   # primary reduce
   if rng.random() < 0.85:
      maxd=min(hi0, 1.0-hi1)
      if maxd <= 1e-9:
         maxd=min(1.0-hi0, hi1)
         if maxd <=1e-9: return None
         direction=1
      else: direction=-1
   else:
      maxd=min(1.0-hi0, hi1)
      if maxd <=1e-9:
         maxd=min(hi0,1.0-hi1)
         if maxd <=1e-9: return None
         direction=-1
   f=float(_FRACTIONS[int(rng.integers(len(_FRACTIONS)))])
   a=direction*maxd*f
   return (i0,i1,a)
```
Note for self direction=-1 means a negative (decrease i). If h[i]>0.5, partner must have room to increase (1-hj). We pick partners h<0.99. Good. If h[i]<0.5, increase i (a positive), partner must have h[j]>0 (decrease), pick partners h>0.01. Good.

`_FRACTIONS` top-level array. Need define before use.

`sample_random_move`:
```
def sample_random_move(h,n,rng):
  i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
  if i==j: return None
  low=max(-float(h[i]), float(h[j])-1.0)
  high=min(1.0-float(h[i]), float(h[j]))
  if low >= high: return None
  # choose a: 50% uniform, 50% toward restoring mean? just uniform
  if rng.random()<0.5:
     a=low + rng.random()*(high-low)
  else:
     # choose random endpoint-ish
     a = low + (high-low)*float(_FRACTIONS[int(rng.integers(len(_FRACTIONS)))])
  if abs(a)<1e-12: return None
  return (i,j,a)
```

def in_budget(deadline):

    return t < deadline
```

def remaining_time(deadline):
   t=time.time()
   rem=deadline-t
   if _RUN_DEADLINE is not None:
      rem=min(rem, _RUN_DEADLINE-t)
   return max(0.0, rem)
```

def slsqp_constraint_eq(x):
    n=max(1,int(x.size))
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5*float(n))
def slsqp_constraint_jac(x):
    n=max(1,int(x.size))

def softmax_weights(S):

   w=np.zeros(S.size)
   act=np.flatnonzero(S >= m - tol)
   if act.size==0: act=np.array([int(np.argmax(S))])

```

`make_feasible` code:
```
def make_feasible(h,n):
   arr=np.array(h,float,copy=True)
   if arr.size != n:
      if arr.size>n: arr=arr[:n]
      else: tmp=np.zeros(n); m=min(n,arr.size); tmp[:m]=arr[:m]; arr=tmp
   arr=np.nan_to_num(arr,nan=0.5,posinf=1.0,neginf=0.0)
   arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1.0)
   target=0.5*float(n)
   s=float(np.sum(arr))
   if abs(s-target) <= 1e-11:
       return arr
   # bisection
   vmin=float(np.min(arr)); vmax=float(np.max(arr))
   lo=-vmax-1.0; hi=1.0-vmin+1.0
   for _ in range(50):
      mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
      s_mid=float(np.sum(np.clip(arr+mid,0.0,1.0)))
      if s_mid < target: lo=mid
      else: hi=mid
   arr=np.clip(arr+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
   return _adjust_residual(arr,target)
```
If arr sum close but tiny >1e-11, bisection; okay.

Adjust function:
```
def _adjust_residual(arr,target):
   diff=target-float(np.sum(arr))
   if abs(diff) < 1e-13: return arr
   # first adjust one/few
   if diff>0:
      idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-arr))
      arr[idx]=min(1.0, arr[idx]+diff)
   else:
      idx=int(np.argmax(arr))
      arr[idx]=max(0.0, arr[idx]+diff)
   for _ in range(arr.size+2):
      diff=target-float(np.sum(arr))
      if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
      if diff>0:
         mask=arr < 1.0 - 1e-15
         if not np.any(mask): break
         cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
         inc=min(diff/float(cnt), float(np.min(1.0-arr[mask])))
         if inc <= 0: break
         arr[mask]+=inc
      else:
         mask=arr > 1e-15
         if not np.any(mask): break
         cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
         dec=min(-diff/float(cnt), float(np.min(arr[mask])))
         if dec <= 0: break
         arr[mask]-=dec
   return np.clip(arr,0.0,1.0)
```
Good.

`binary_flip_update` in previous flips h[i] from old to new_val and updates scores d. It assumes h[i] changes by 0/1. Let's verify formula with directed counts. For flip at i to new_val, for each d:
- Consider pidx plus: pairs (x,x+d).
  If i-d >=0: pair r=i-d includes h_i as second factor. If new_val=1: contribution changes from old second 0/1? Previous code:
    hj = int(h[i-d]) (old value of other first factor)
    if new_val==1: scores[pidx] -= hj; scores[midx] += 1 - hj
    else: scores[pidx] += hj; scores[midx] -= 1 - hj
Let's derive: plus score term h_{i-d}(1-h_i). Old depends on old h_i. If new=1, term becomes 0; if old=0 term was h_{i-d}; if old=1 term 0. Increase new - old = 1: change = -h_{i-d}. So new_val 1 => scores[pidx] -= hj. If new=0, term becomes h_{i-d}; if old=1 term 0; change +h_{i-d}. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_RUN_DEADLINE = None
_GLOBAL_DEADLINE = None
_FRACTIONS = np.array([0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00], dtype=float)


class BudgetError(Exception):
    pass


def check_deadline():
    if _RUN_DEADLINE is not None and time.time() > _RUN_DEADLINE:
        raise BudgetError


def in_budget(deadline):
    t = time.time()
    if _RUN_DEADLINE is not None and t >= _RUN_DEADLINE:
        return False
    return t < deadline


def remaining_time(deadline):
    t = time.time()
    rem = float(deadline - t)
    if _RUN_DEADLINE is not None:
        rem = min(rem, float(_RUN_DEADLINE - t))
    return max(0.0, rem)


def _adjust_residual(arr, target):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=float).copy()
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = float(target - np.sum(arr))
    if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
        return arr
    if diff > 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - arr))
        arr[idx] = min(1.0, float(arr[idx]) + diff)
    else:
        idx = int(np.argmax(arr))
        arr[idx] = max(0.0, float(arr[idx]) + diff)
    for _ in range(arr.size + 2):
        diff = float(target - np.sum(arr))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = arr < (1.0 - 1e-15)
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            inc = min(diff / float(cnt), float(np.min(1.0 - arr[mask])))
            if inc <= 0.0:
                break
            arr[mask] += inc
        else:
            mask = arr > 1e-15
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            dec = min(-diff / float(cnt), float(np.min(arr[mask])))
            if dec <= 0.0:
                break
            arr[mask] -= dec
    return np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1.0)


def make_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    n = int(n)
    if h.size != n:
        if h.size > n:
            h = h[:n]
        else:
            tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            m = min(n, h.size)
            if m > 0:
                tmp[:m] = h[:m]
            h = tmp
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    s = float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(s - target) <= 1e-11:
        return h
    vmin = float(np.min(h)) if h.size > 0 else 0.0
    vmax = float(np.max(h)) if h.size > 0 else 1.0
    lo = -vmax - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - vmin + 1.0
    for _ in range(50):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        sm = float(np.sum(np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if sm < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    return _adjust_residual(h, target)


def score_sum(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=float)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(h[0] * (1.0 - h[0]))], dtype=float)
    ac = np.correlate(h, h, mode="full")
    pref = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(h, out=pref[1:])
    total = float(pref[n])
    S = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    S[0] = total - float(ac[n - 1])
    d = np.arange(1, n, dtype=int)
    m = n - d
    a = ac[n - 1 + d]
    S[1:2 * n - 1:2] = pref[m] - a
    S[2:2 * n:2] = total - pref[d] - a
    return S


def compute_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return 1e6
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    conv = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    c = float(np.max(conv)) * (2.0 / float(n))
    if not math.isfinite(c):
        return 1e6
    return c


class OptimizerState:
    def __init__(self, h, c, n):
        self.h = None
        self.c = 1e18
        self.n = 0
        self.update(h, c, n)

    def update(self, h, c, n):
        try:
            h2 = make_feasible(h, int(n))
            c2 = compute_c5(h2)
        except Exception:
            return
        if math.isfinite(c2) and c2 < self.c - 1e-14:
            self.h = h2
            self.c = float(c2)
            self.n = int(n)

    def update_fast(self, h, c, n):
        try:
            n = int(n)
            h2 = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
            if h2.size != n:
                if h2.size > n:
                    h2 = h2[:n]
                else:
                    tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
                    m = min(n, h2.size)
                    if m > 0:
                        tmp[:m] = h2[:m]
                    h2 = tmp
            h2 = np.nan_to_num(h2, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
            h2 = np.clip(h2, 0.0, 1.0)
            target = 0.5 * float(n)
            if abs(float(np.sum(h2)) - target) > 1e-6:
                h2 = make_feasible(h2, n)
        except Exception:
            return
        if math.isfinite(c) and c < self.c - 1e-12:
            self.h = h2
            self.c = float(c)
            self.n = int(n)


def get_parent():
    try:
        p = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if p.ndim != 1 or p.size < 2:
            return None
        return p.copy()
    except Exception:
        return None


def upsample_linear(parent, n):
    parent = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float)
    n = int(n)
    if parent.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
    if parent.size == n:
        return parent.copy()
    x0 = (np.arange(parent.size) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(parent.size))
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    return np.interp(x, x0, parent).astype(float)


def antisyms(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return 0.5 * (h + (1.0 - h[::-1].copy()))


def random_wave_h(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n)) - 1.0
    y = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for f in (1, 2, 3, 5):
        y += float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0 / float(f))) * np.sin(
            math.pi * float(f) * x + float(rng.normal(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        )
    h = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.tanh(y)
    return make_feasible(h, n)


def random_block_h(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    i = 0
    bit = int(rng.integers(2))
    min_len = 2
    max_len = max(min_len + 1, int(min(10, max(3, n // 4))))
    while i < n:
        length = int(rng.integers(min_len, max_len + 1))
        if i + length > n:
            length = n - i
        if length <= 0:
            break
        h[i:i + length] = float(bit)
        i += length
        bit = 1 - bit
    kernel = np.array([0.20, 0.60, 0.20], dtype=float)
    try:
        out = np.convolve(h, kernel, mode="same")[:n]
    except Exception:
        out = h
    return make_feasible(out, n)


def project_box_sum(v, target):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    n = int(v.size)
    target = float(target)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if target <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if target >= float(n):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    vmin = float(np.min(v))
    vmax = float(np.max(v))
    lo = -vmax - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - vmin + 1.0
    for _ in range(38):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        sm = float(np.sum(np.clip(v + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if sm < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    z = np.clip(v + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    return _adjust_residual(z, target)


def softmax_weights(S, alpha):
    S = np.asarray(S, dtype=float)
    m = float(np.max(S))
    L = S.size
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        if L > 0:
            w[int(np.argmax(S if np.all(np.isfinite(S)) else np.zeros(L)))] = 1.0
        return w
    if alpha <= 1e-13:
        tol = max(1e-9, 1e-7 * abs(m))
        act = np.flatnonzero(S >= (m - tol))
        if act.size == 0:
            act = np.array([int(np.argmax(S))])
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        w[act] = 1.0 / float(act.size)
        return w
    z = (S - m) / float(alpha)
    p = np.exp(z)
    ps = float(np.sum(p))
    if (not math.isfinite(ps)) or ps <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(S))] = 1.0
        return w
    p = p / ps
    idx = np.flatnonzero(p > 1e-9)
    if idx.size == 0:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(S))] = 1.0
        return w
    pref = np.cumsum(p)
    th = float(rng_global_exp.next_double())
    r = int(np.searchsorted(pref, th, side="left"))
    if r >= L:
        r = L - 1
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    w[r] = 1.0
    return w


class _RNG:
    def __init__(self, rng):
        self.rng = rng

    def next_double(self):
        return float(self.rng.random())


rng_global_exp = _RNG(np.random.default_rng(0))


def add_plus_grad(G, w, h, d, m):
    if w == 0.0:
        return
    G[:m] += w * (1.0 - h[d:])
    G[d:] -= w * h[:m]


def add_minus_grad(G, w, h, d, m):
    if w == 0.0:
        return
    G[d:] += w * (1.0 - h[:m])
    G[:m] -= w * h[d:]


def score_and_gradient(h, alpha):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    n = int(h.size)
    S = score_sum(h)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(S)):
        return S, 1e6, np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    m = float(np.max(S))
    if alpha > 1e-13:
        z = (S - m) / float(alpha)
        p = np.exp(z)
        ps = float(np.sum(p))
        if not math.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 0.0:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(S))], dtype=int)
            wts = np.array([1.0], dtype=float)
        else:
            p = p / ps
            idx = np.flatnonzero(p > 1e-9)
            if idx.size == 0:
                idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(S))], dtype=int)
                wts = np.array([1.0], dtype=float)
            else:
                wts = p[idx].copy()
                ws = float(np.sum(wts))
                if ws <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(ws):
                    idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(S))], dtype=int)
                    wts = np.array([1.0], dtype=float)
                else:
                    wts = wts / ws
    else:
        tol = max(1e-9, 1e-7 * abs(m))
        idx = np.flatnonzero(S >= (m - tol))
        if idx.size == 0:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(S))], dtype=int)
        wts = np.full(idx.size, 1.0 / float(idx.size), dtype=float)
    G = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for ii, kk in enumerate(idx):
        k = int(kk)
        w = float(wts[ii])
        if w <= 1e-12:
            continue
        if k == 0:
            G += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        else:
            if k % 2 == 1:
                d = (k + 1) // 2
            else:
                d = k // 2
            m = n - d
            if m <= 0:
                continue
            if k % 2 == 1:
                add_plus_grad(G, w, h, d, m)
            else:
                add_minus_grad(G, w, h, d, m)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)):
        G = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    return S, m, G


def slsqp_objective(x, alpha):
    check_deadline()
    h = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return 1e6
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    S, m, _ = score_and_gradient(h, 0.0)
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return 1e6
    if alpha <= 1e-13:
        return float(m)
    S = np.asarray(S, dtype=float)
    z = (S - m) / float(alpha)
    p = np.exp(z)
    A = float(np.sum(p))
    if not math.isfinite(A) or A <= 0.0:
        return float(m)
    val = float(m) + float(alpha) * math.log(A)
    if not math.isfinite(val):
        return 1e6
    return val


def slsqp_gradient(x, alpha):
    check_deadline()
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    _, _, G = score_and_gradient(h, float(alpha))
    return G


def slsqp_constraint_eq(x):
    n = max(1, int(x.size))
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(n))


def slsqp_constraint_jac(x):
    n = max(1, int(x.size))
    return np.ones((1, n), dtype=float)


def slsqp_phases_moderate(n):
    if n <= 80:
        return [(0.30, 120), (0.12, 160), (0.05, 200),
                (0.02, 200), (0.008, 180), (0.002, 140)]
    if n <= 180:
        return [(0.30, 80), (0.12, 100), (0.05, 120),
                (0.02, 120), (0.008, 100), (0.002, 70)]
    return [(0.30, 50), (0.12, 60), (0.05, 80),
            (0.02, 80), (0.008, 60), (0.002, 50)]


def slsqp_phases_final(n):
    if n <= 80:
        return [(0.02, 130), (0.008, 130), (0.002, 140), (0.0, 100)]
    if n <= 180:
        return [(0.02, 90), (0.008, 90), (0.002, 100), (0.0, 70)]
    return [(0.02, 55), (0.008, 55), (0.002, 60), (0.0, 45)]


def slsqp_polish(h, n, state, deadline, time_limit, phases=None):
    global _RUN_DEADLINE
    n = int(n)
    h = make_feasible(h, n)
    cur_U = float(np.max(score_sum(h)))
    cur_C = cur_U * (2.0 / float(n))
    best_U = cur_U
    best_h = h.copy()
    state.update_fast(best_h, best_U * 2.0 / float(n), n)
    if phases is None:
        phases = slsqp_phases_moderate(n)
    stale = 0
    for alpha, base_iter in phases:
        rem = remaining_time(deadline)
        if rem < 12.0:
            break
        maxiter = int(base_iter)
        if n > 160:
            maxiter = max(20, maxiter // 2)
        maxiter = max(20, maxiter)
        old_dl = _RUN_DEADLINE
        stage_dl = min(old_dl, deadline) if old_dl is not None else deadline
        _RUN_DEADLINE = min(stage_dl, time.time() + rem - 2.0)
        res = None
        try:
            bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
            constraints = [
                {"type": "eq", "fun": slsqp_constraint_eq, "jac": slsqp_constraint_jac}
            ]
            res = minimize(
                slsqp_objective,
                h,
                args=(float(alpha),),
                jac=slsqp_gradient,
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=constraints,
                options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-12, "disp": 0},
            )
        except BudgetError:
            res = None
        except Exception:
            res = None
        finally:
            _RUN_DEADLINE = old_dl
        if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
            stale += 1
            if stale >= 2:
                break
            continue
        h2 = make_feasible(res.x, n)
        U2 = float(np.max(score_sum(h2)))
        C2 = U2 * (2.0 / float(n))
        if U2 < cur_U + 1e-10 or U2 <= cur_U + 0.08:
            h = h2
            cur_U = U2
            cur_C = C2
        if U2 < best_U - 1e-12:
            best_U = U2
            best_h = h2.copy()
            stale = 0
        else:
            stale += 1
        state.update_fast(best_h, best_U * 2.0 / float(n), n)
        if stale >= 3:
            break
    state.update(best_h, best_U * 2.0 / float(n), n)
    return best_h, best_U


def pg_refine(h, n, state, deadline, time_limit, rng):
    n = int(n)
    h = make_feasible(h, n)
    S = score_sum(h)
    U = float(np.max(S))
    best_U = U
    best_h = h.copy()
    state.update_fast(best_h, best_U * 2.0 / float(n), n)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    alphas = [0.25, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015, 0.008]
    eta = 0.16
    for alpha in alphas:
        it = 0
        no_loc = 0
        last_best = time.time()
        while it < 2600 and in_budget(deadline):
            it += 1
            S = score_sum(h)
            U = float(np.max(S))
            C = U * (2.0 / float(n))
            if U < best_U - 1e-10:
                best_U = U
                best_h = h.copy()
                state.update_fast(best_h, C, n)
                last_best = time.time()
            if time.time() - last_best > 18.0:
                break
            S, _, G = score_and_gradient(h, float(alpha))
            G = np.asarray(G, dtype=float)
            normG = float(np.linalg.norm(G))
            if not math.isfinite(normG) or normG <= 1e-11:
                break
            step = eta / max(0.7, normG)
            improved = False
            for _ in range(5):
                z = project_box_sum(h - step * G, target)
                S2 = score_sum(z)
                U2 = float(np.max(S2))
                if math.isfinite(U2) and U2 < U - 1e-9:
                    h = z
                    S = S2
                    U = U2
                    C = U * (2.0 / float(n))
                    if U < best_U - 1e-10:
                        best_U = U
                        best_h = h.copy()
                        state.update_fast(best_h, C, n)
                    eta = min(0.85, eta * 1.12)
                    improved = True
                    break
                step *= 0.5
            if improved:
                no_loc = 0
            else:
                no_loc += 1
                eta = max(0.025, eta * 0.75)
                if no_loc in (90, 200, 400):
                    p = np.asarray(rng.normal(0.0, 0.035, n), dtype=float)
                    p -= float(np.mean(p))
                    h = project_box_sum(h + p, target)
                    S = score_sum(h)
                    U = float(np.max(S))
                    no_loc = 0
                if no_loc > 700:
                    break
            if U > best_U + 0.35:
                h = best_h.copy()
                S = score_sum(h)
                U = float(np.max(S))
    state.update(best_h, best_U * 2.0 / float(n), n)
    return best_h


def update_scores_pair(S, h, i, j, a, n):
    a = float(a)
    if a == 0.0:
        return
    b = -a
    hi = float(h[i])
    hj = float(h[j])
    S[0] += a * (1.0 - 2.0 * hi) + b * (1.0 - 2.0 * hj) - a * a - b * b
    for d in range(1, n):
        m = n - d
        dp = 0.0
        if i < m:
            dp += a * (1.0 - float(h[i + d]))
        if i >= d:
            dp -= a * float(h[i - d])
        if j < m:
            dp += b * (1.0 - float(h[j + d]))
        if j >= d:
            dp -= b * float(h[j - d])
        ip = 2 * d - 1
        S[ip] += dp
        dm = 0.0
        if i >= d:
            dm += a * (1.0 - float(h[i - d]))
        if i < m:
            dm -= a * float(h[i + d])
        if j >= d:
            dm += b * (1.0 - float(h[j - d]))
        if j < m:
            dm -= b * float(h[j + d])
        S[ip + 1] += dm
        if (j - i == d) or (i - j == d):
            cross = -a * b
            S[ip] += cross
            S[ip + 1] += cross


def sample_random_move(h, n, rng):
    i = int(rng.integers(n))
    j = int(rng.integers(n))
    if i == j:
        return None
    low = max(-float(h[i]), float(h[j]) - 1.0)
    high = min(1.0 - float(h[i]), float(h[j]))
    if low >= high - 1e-12:
        return None
    if rng.random() < 0.55:
        a = low + float(rng.random() * (high - low))
    else:
        a = low + float(_FRACTIONS[int(rng.integers(len(_FRACTIONS)))]) * (high - low)
    if abs(a) < 1e-12:
        return None
    return i, j, a


def sample_target_move(h, S, U, n, act, rng):
    if act is None or act.size == 0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(S))
    else:
        idx = int(act[int(rng.integers(act.size))])
    if idx == 0:
        c = h * (1.0 - h)
        pos = np.flatnonzero(c > 1e-10)
        if pos.size == 0:
            return None
        i = int(pos[int(rng.integers(pos.size))])
        if float(h[i]) > 0.5:
            partners = np.flatnonzero(h < 0.99)
            direction = -1.0
        else:
            partners = np.flatnonzero(h > 0.01)
            direction = 1.0
        if partners.size == 0:
            return None
        j = int(partners[int(rng.integers(partners.size))])
        if i == j:
            return None
        if direction < 0.0:
            maxd = min(float(h[i]), 1.0 - float(h[j]))
        else:
            maxd = min(1.0 - float(h[i]), float(h[j]))
        if maxd <= 1e-8:
            return None
        a = direction * maxd * float(_FRACTIONS[int(rng.integers(len(_FRACTIONS)))])
        return i, j, a
    if idx % 2 == 1:
        d = (idx + 1) // 2
    else:
        d = idx // 2
    m = n - d
    if m <= 0:
        return None
    if idx % 2 == 1:
        c = h[:m] * (1.0 - h[d:])
    else:
        c = h[d:] * (1.0 - h[:m])
    tot = float(np.sum(c))
    if not math.isfinite(tot) or tot <= 1e-12:
        return None
    cs = np.cumsum(c)
    r = int(np.searchsorted(cs, float(rng.random() * tot), side="left"))
    if r >= m:
        r = m - 1
    if idx % 2 == 1:
        i0 = int(r)
        i1 = int(r + d)
    else:
        i0 = int(r + d)
        i1 = int(r)
    hi0 = float(h[i0])
    hi1 = float(h[i1])
    if rng.random() < 0.85:
        maxd = min(hi0, 1.0 - hi1)
        direction = -1.0
        if maxd <= 1e-9:
            maxd = min(1.0 - hi0, hi1)
            direction = 1.0
    else:
        maxd = min(1.0 - hi0, hi1)
        direction = 1.0
        if maxd <= 1e-9:
            maxd = min(hi0, 1.0 - hi1)
            direction = -1.0
    if maxd <= 1e-9:
        return None
    a = direction * maxd * float(_FRACTIONS[int(rng.integers(len(_FRACTIONS)))])
    return i0, i1, a


def targeted_local_search(h0, n, state, deadline, rng):
    n = int(n)
    h = make_feasible(h0, n)
    S = score_sum(h)
    U = float(np.max(S))
    best_U = U
    best_h = h.copy()
    state.update_fast(best_h, best_U * 2.0 / float(n), n)
    act = None
    act_counter = 0
    no_improve = 0
    accepted = 0
    while in_budget(deadline):
        if act is None or no_improve >= 80 or act_counter > 350:
            act_tol = max(0.02, 0.0025 * float(U))
            act = np.flatnonzero(S >= (U - act_tol))
            if act.size == 0:
                act = np.array([int(np.argmax(S))], dtype=int)
            act_counter = 0
        best_move = None
        best_U_test = U + 1e-8
        best_S_test = None
        escape_move = None
        escape_U = U
        escape_S = None
        for _ in range(6):
            if rng.random() < 0.78:
                mv = sample_target_move(h, S, U, n, act, rng)
            else:
                mv = sample_random_move(h, n, rng)
            if mv is None:
                continue
            i, j, a = mv
            low = max(-float(h[i]), float(h[j]) - 1.0)
            high = min(1.0 - float(h[i]), float(h[j]))
            if low >= high - 1e-12:
                continue
            a = min(max(float(a), low), high)
            if abs(a) < 1e-12:
                continue
            S_test = S.copy()
            update_scores_pair(S_test, h, i, j, a, n)
            newU = float(np.max(S_test))
            if not math.isfinite(newU):
                continue
            if newU < best_U_test:
                best_U_test = newU
                best_move = (i, j, a)
                best_S_test = S_test
            if newU < U + max(0.06, 0.004 * float(U)):
                escape_move = (i, j, a)
                escape_U = newU
                escape_S = S_test
        if best_move is not None and best_U_test < U - 1e-9:
            i, j, a = best_move
            h[i] = min(1.0, max(0.0, float(h[i]) + a))
            h[j] = min(1.0, max(0.0, float(h[j]) - a))
            S = best_S_test
            U = best_U_test
            C = U * 2.0 / float(n)
            if U < best_U - 1e-10:
                best_U = U
                best_h = h.copy()
            state.update_fast(best_h, best_U * 2.0 / float(n), n)
            no_improve = 0
            act = None
            act_counter += 1
            accepted += 1
            if accepted % 180 == 0:
                S = score_sum(h)
                U = float(np.max(S))
                C = U * 2.0 / float(n)
                if U < best_U - 1e-10:
                    best_U = U
                    best_h = h.copy()
                state.update_fast(best_h, best_U * 2.0 / float(n), n)
        elif no_improve > 160 and escape_move is not None:
            i, j, a = escape_move
            h[i] = min(1.0, max(0.0, float(h[i]) + a))
            h[j] = min(1.0, max(0.0, float(h[j]) - a))
            S = escape_S
            U = escape_U
            no_improve = 0
            act = None
            act_counter += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 1600:
                h = best_h.copy()
                S = score_sum(h)
                U = best_U
                no_improve = 0
                act = None
    state.update(best_h, best_U * 2.0 / float(n), n)
    return best_h


def make_binary_area(start, n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    target = n // 2
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    if target <= 0:
        return h
    if start is not None:
        s = np.asarray(start, dtype=float)
        if s.size == n:
            vals = s.copy()
            vals = np.nan_to_num(vals, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
            vals = np.clip(vals, 0.0, 1.0)
            order = np.argsort(vals)
            h[order[target:]] = 1
            return h
    idx = rng.choice(n, size=target, replace=False)
    h[idx] = 1
    return h


def binary_scores(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    S = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    hf = h.astype(float)
    for d in range(1, n):
        S[2 * d - 1] = float(np.dot(hf[:n - d], 1.0 - hf[d:]))
        S[2 * d] = float(np.dot(hf[d:], 1.0 - hf[:n - d]))
    return S


def binary_flip_update(scores, h, idx, new_val, n):
    idx = int(idx)
    new_val = int(new_val)
    i = idx
    for d in range(1, n):
        pidx = 2 * d - 1
        midx = 2 * d
        if i - d >= 0:
            hj = int(h[i - d])
            if new_val == 1:
                scores[pidx] -= hj
                scores[midx] += 1.0 - hj
            else:
                scores[pidx] += hj
                scores[midx] -= 1.0 - hj
        if i + d < n:
            hj = int(h[i + d])
            if new_val == 1:
                scores[pidx] += 1.0 - hj
                scores[midx] -= hj
            else:
                scores[pidx] -= 1.0 - hj
                scores[midx] += hj
    h[i] = new_val


def binary_swap_apply(scores, h, i, j, n):
    i = int(i)
    j = int(j)
    if i == j:
        return False
    hi = int(h[i])
    hj = int(h[j])
    if hi == hj:
        return False
    if hi == 0 and hj == 1:
        binary_flip_update(scores, h, i, 1, n)
        binary_flip_update(scores, h, j, 0, n)
    else:
        binary_flip_update(scores, h, i, 0, n)
        binary_flip_update(scores, h, j, 1, n)
    return True


def propose_binary_swap(h, scores, cur, rng, n):
    if rng.random() < 0.72:
        act = np.flatnonzero(scores >= cur - 1e-9)
        if act.size > 0:
            idx = int(act[int(rng.integers(act.size))])
            if idx != 0:
                if idx % 2 == 1:
                    d = (idx + 1) // 2
                    mask = (h[:n - d] == 1) & (h[d:] == 0)
                else:
                    d = idx // 2
                    mask = (h[:n - d] == 0) & (h[d:] == 1)
                cand = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
                if cand.size > 0:
                    pos = int(cand[int(rng.integers(cand.size))])
                    return pos, pos + d
    zeros = np.flatnonzero(h == 0)
    ones = np.flatnonzero(h == 1)
    if zeros.size == 0 or ones.size == 0:
        return -1, -1
    i = int(zeros[int(rng.integers(zeros.size))])
    j = int(ones[int(rng.integers(ones.size))])
    return i, j


def binary_local_once(start_h, n, state, rng, deadline, max_ops, stall_limit):
    n = int(n)
    h = make_binary_area(start_h, n, rng).astype(np.int8)
    scores = binary_scores(h, n)
    cur = float(np.max(scores))
    best = cur
    best_h = h.copy()
    if state is not None:
        state.update_fast(h.astype(float), best * (2.0 / float(n)), n)
    ops = 0
    stall = 0
    while ops < max_ops and in_budget(deadline):
        ops += 1
        i, j = propose_binary_swap(h, scores, cur, rng, n)
        if i < 0:
            stall += 40
            if stall > stall_limit:
                break
            continue
        if not binary_swap_apply(scores, h, i, j, n):
            continue
        new_cur = float(np.max(scores))
        if new_cur + 1e-9 < cur:
            cur = new_cur
            stall = 0
            if cur < best:
                best = cur
                best_h = h.copy()
                if state is not None:
                    state.update_fast(h.astype(float), best * (2.0 / float(n)), n)
        elif new_cur <= cur + 1e-9:
            binary_swap_apply(scores, h, i, j, n)
            stall += 1
        else:
            binary_swap_apply(scores, h, i, j, n)
            stall += 1
        if stall > stall_limit:
            break
    final_c = cur * (2.0 / float(n))
    if state is not None:
        state.update_fast(h.astype(float), final_c, n)
    if final_c < best + 1e-12:
        best_h = h.copy()
        best = final_c
    return best_h.astype(float), float(best * (2.0 / float(n)))


def binary_hill_search(start_h, n, state, deadline, time_limit, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return None, 1e9
    stage_dl = time.time() + max(1.0, float(time_limit))
    if deadline is not None:
        stage_dl = min(stage_dl, float(deadline))
    if _RUN_DEADLINE is not None:
        stage_dl = min(stage_dl, float(_RUN_DEADLINE))
    best_h = None
    best_c = 1e9
    max_restarts = 3 if n <= 120 else 2
    max_ops = 70000 if n <= 100 else 28000
    stall_limit = 4500 if n <= 100 else 2200
    restart_time = max(4.0, float(time_limit) / float(max_restarts))
    done = 0
    while done < max_restarts and time.time() < stage_dl - 1.5:
        if done == 0 and start_h is not None:
            start = start_h
        else:
            start = None
        rl = time.time() + restart_time
        if rl > stage_dl:
            rl = stage_dl
        try:
            hh, cc = binary_local_once(start, n, state, rng, rl, max_ops, stall_limit)
        except Exception:
            hh, cc = None, 1e9
        if hh is not None and math.isfinite(cc) and cc < best_c:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hh
        done += 1
    return best_h, best_c


def is_same_candidate(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float)
    if a.size != b.size:
        return False
    return bool(np.allclose(a, b, rtol=1e-7, atol=1e-7))


def candidate_not_duplicate(cands, h):
    for hh, _ in cands:
        if is_same_candidate(hh, h):
            return False
    return True


def add_candidate(h, n, cands):
    try:
        h2 = make_feasible(h, int(n))
        c2 = compute_c5(h2)
        if math.isfinite(c2) and c2 < 1e5 and candidate_not_duplicate(cands, h2):
            cands.append((h2, float(c2)))
    except Exception:
        pass


def cand_key(item):
    return item[1]


def refine_candidate(h0, n, state, deadline, time_limit, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 4:
        return
    h = make_feasible(h0, n)
    U = float(np.max(score_sum(h)))
    state.update_fast(h, U * 2.0 / float(n), n)

    rem = remaining_time(deadline)
    if rem > 90.0:
        pg_t = min(55.0, 0.18 * float(time_limit))
        pg_dl = min(deadline, time.time() + pg_t)
        h = pg_refine(h, n, state, pg_dl, pg_t, rng)

    rem = remaining_time(deadline)
    if rem > 45.0:
        slq_t = min(210.0, 0.45 * float(time_limit))
        slq_dl = min(deadline, time.time() + slq_t)
        h, _ = slsqp_polish(h, n, state, slq_dl, slq_t, slsqp_phases_moderate(n))

    rem = remaining_time(deadline)
    if rem > 35.0:
        tl_t = min(150.0, 0.38 * float(time_limit))
        tl_dl = min(deadline, time.time() + tl_t)
        h = targeted_local_search(h, n, state, tl_dl, rng)

    rem = remaining_time(deadline)
    if rem > 35.0:
        slq_t = min(120.0, 0.30 * float(time_limit))
        slq_dl = min(deadline, time.time() + slq_t)
        h, _ = slsqp_polish(h, n, state, slq_dl, slq_t, slsqp_phases_final(n))

    rem = remaining_time(deadline)
    if rem > 20.0:
        tl_t = min(80.0, 0.25 * float(time_limit))
        tl_dl = min(deadline, time.time() + tl_t)
        h = targeted_local_search(h, n, state, tl_dl, rng)

    h = make_feasible(h, n)
    U = float(np.max(score_sum(h)))
    state.update(h, U * 2.0 / float(n), n)
    return h


def optimize_n(h_main, n, state, deadline, time_limit, rng):
    global _RUN_DEADLINE
    n = int(n)
    stage_dl = min(deadline, time.time() + float(time_limit))
    rem = remaining_time(stage_dl)
    if rem < 25.0:
        add_candidate(h_main, n, [])
        return

    cands = []
    add_candidate(h_main, n, cands)

    if state is not None and state.h is not None:
        if state.n == n:
            add_candidate(state.h, n, cands)
        else:
            add_candidate(upsample_linear(state.h, n), n, cands)

    if cands:
        add_candidate(antisyms(cands[0][0]), n, cands)

    add_candidate(random_block_h(n, rng), n, cands)
    add_candidate(random_wave_h(n, rng), n, cands)

    if n % 2 == 0 and cands:
        seed_bin = cands[0][0]
        h_bin = make_binary_area(seed_bin, n, rng)
        add_candidate(h_bin.astype(float), n, cands)
        rem = remaining_time(stage_dl)
        if rem > 90.0:
            bin_t = min(35.0, 0.12 * float(time_limit))
            phase_end = min(stage_dl, time.time() + bin_t)
            old_dl = _RUN_DEADLINE
            new_dl = min(old_dl, phase_end) if old_dl is not None else phase_end
            _RUN_DEADLINE = new_dl
            try:
                bh, bc = binary_hill_search(h_bin, n, state, phase_end, bin_t, rng)
                if bh is not None:
                    add_candidate(bh, n, cands)
            except Exception:
                pass
            finally:
                _RUN_DEADLINE = old_dl

    if state is not None and state.h is not None and state.n == n:
        add_candidate(state.h, n, cands)

    if cands:
        cands.sort(key=cand_key)
    else:
        h = make_feasible(np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float), n)
        c = compute_c5(h)
        cands.append((h, c))
        state.update(h, c, n)

    rem = remaining_time(stage_dl)
    if rem < 30.0:
        for hh, cc in cands:
            state.update(hh, cc, n)
        return

    if rem > 520.0 and len(cands) >= 3:
        starts = [cands[0], cands[1], cands[2]]
    elif rem > 260.0 and len(cands) >= 2:
        starts = [cands[0], cands[1]]
    else:
        starts = [cands[0]]

    if len(starts) == 1:
        fracs = [1.0]
    elif len(starts) == 2:
        fracs = [0.66, 0.34]
    else:
        fracs = [0.52, 0.31, 0.17]

    for hh, frac in zip(starts, fracs):
        rem = remaining_time(stage_dl)
        if rem < 25.0:
            break
        tt = min(rem - 10.0, max(25.0, float(frac) * rem))
        tt = max(20.0, tt)
        dl = min(stage_dl, time.time() + tt)
        try:
            refine_candidate(hh[0], n, state, dl, tt, rng)
        except Exception:
            pass

    for hh, cc in cands:
        state.update(hh, cc, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    global _RUN_DEADLINE, _GLOBAL_DEADLINE
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 1300.0
    if not math.isfinite(budget_s) or budget_s <= 60.0:
        budget_s = 60.0
    reserve = 35.0
    global_dl = t0 + max(1.0, budget_s - reserve)
    _GLOBAL_DEADLINE = global_dl
    _RUN_DEADLINE = global_dl

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    rng_global_exp.__init__(rng)

    parent = get_parent()
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        n_init = int(parent.size)
        h_init = make_feasible(parent, n_init)
    else:
        n_init = 70
        h_init = random_wave_h(n_init, rng)
    c_init = compute_c5(h_init)
    state = OptimizerState(h_init, c_init, n_init)

    base = n_init
    if base % 2 != 0:
        base += 1
    base = max(24, min(base, 240))
    ns = []
    for nn in (base, 2 * base, base + 20, base + 40, 4 * base):
        if nn % 2 != 0:
            nn -= 1
        if nn >= 24 and nn <= max(480, 4 * base) and nn not in ns:
            ns.append(nn)
    if len(ns) == 0:
        ns = [base]
    if len(ns) > 3:
        ns = ns[:3]

    first_time = max(300.0, 0.58 * (budget_s - reserve)) if len(ns) >= 2 else max(300.0, 0.8 * (budget_s - reserve))
    first_lim = min(first_time, budget_s - reserve - 60.0)
    h_main0 = parent.copy() if (parent is not None and parent.size == ns[0]) else upsample_linear(h_init, ns[0])
    h_main0 = make_feasible(h_main0, ns[0])
    try:
        optimize_n(h_main0, ns[0], state, global_dl, first_lim, rng)
    except Exception:
        pass

    rem = remaining_time(global_dl)
    for k in range(1, len(ns)):
        if rem < 90.0:
            break
        nn = int(ns[k])
        hh = make_feasible(upsample_linear(state.h, nn), nn)
        lim = max(60.0, min(rem - 40.0, 0.52 * rem))
        try:
            optimize_n(hh, nn, state, global_dl, lim, rng)
        except Exception:
            pass
        rem = remaining_time(global_dl)

    rem = remaining_time(global_dl)
    if rem > 80.0 and state.n >= 4:
        h_last = make_feasible(state.h, state.n)
        lim = min(rem - 25.0, 170.0)
        try:
            refine_candidate(h_last, state.n, state, global_dl, lim, rng)
        except Exception:
            pass

    h_final = make_feasible(state.h, state.n)
    c_final = compute_c5(h_final)
    n_final = int(state.n)
    if not math.isfinite(c_final) or c_final > 1e5:
        h_final = make_feasible(h_init, n_init)
        c_final = compute_c5(h_final)
        n_final = int(n_init)
    state.update(h_final, c_final, n_final)
    return h_final, float(c_final), int(n_final)
```
